AC-001-03-01; BR-002\n- **Verification.** `residency.ci_gate.passes_all_eu` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCY-685134`\n\n**BE-RESIDENCY-FR-007 \u2014 CI gate fails on any non-EU target**\n- Given a deployment with at least one target resource outside the EU, when the gate is evaluated, then the verdict is `fail`, the build fails and the deployment is blocked.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-03; AC-001-03-02; BR-002; NFR-002\n- **Verification.** `residency.ci_gate.fails_any_non_eu` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCY-685134`\n\n**BE-RESIDENCY-FR-008 \u2014 CI gate fails closed on missing region metadata**\n- Given a target resource whose region metadata is missing or unreadable, when the gate is evaluated, then that resource is `fail` and the verdict is `fail`. It never passes by default.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-03; AC-001-03-03\n- **Verification.** `residency.ci_gate.fails_on_missing_region` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCY-685134`\n\n**BE-RESIDENCY-FR-009 \u2014 Compliance report content**\n- Given all resources exist and have been scanned, when the report is requested, then it lists each resource with its region and a `pass`, `fail` or `unverified` status, plus an overall percent compliant.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-04; AC-001-04-01; NFR-001; NFR-004\n- **Verification.** `residency.report.lists_all_resources_with_percent` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCY-913995`\n\n**BE-RESIDENCY-FR-010 \u2014 Unrecognised resource type is flagged, not omitted**\n- Given a resource whose type the scanner does not recognise, when the report is generated, then the resource appears with status `unverified`.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-04; AC-001-04-02\n- **Verification.** `residency.report.flags_unrecognised_type_unverified` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCY-913995`\n\n**BE-RESIDENCY-FR-011 \u2014 Launch gate passes at 100%**\n- Given the refreshed report shows 100% EU-verified resources, when the launch gate check runs, then the gate is marked passed and release is authorised.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-05; AC-001-05-01; BR-003\n- **Verification.** `residency.launch_gate.passes_at_100_percent` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCY-648672`\n\n**BE-RESIDENCY-FR-012 \u2014 Launch gate fails below 100%**\n- Given the refreshed report shows less than 100% EU-verified resources (an `unverified` resource counts as not verified, `ASM-BE-265`), when the launch gate check runs, then the gate is marked failed and release is blocked until a later check passes.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-05; AC-001-05-02; BR-003\n- **Verification.** `residency.launch_gate.fails_below_100_percent` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCY-648672`\n\n**BE-RESIDENCY-FR-013 \u2014 Launch gate refreshes the report**\n- Given a launch gate check is requested, when it runs, then it scans the full inventory first, so the report it reads is current.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-05; NFR-004 (\"Report refreshed and 100% accurate at each launch gate check\")\n- **Verification.** `residency.launch_gate.refreshes_report_before_verdict` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCY-648672`; `API-RESIDENCY-913995`\n\n**BE-RESIDENCY-FR-014 \u2014 Clean drift scan raises no alert**\n- Given a drift scan runs and every resource is in an EU region, when it completes, then no alert is raised and the scan logs a pass.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-06; AC-001-06-01\n- **Verification.** `residency.drift.pass_raises_no_alert` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCY-922826`\n\n**BE-RESIDENCY-FR-015 \u2014 Drift raises an alert within 15 minutes**\n- Given a drift scan finds a resource in a non-EU region, when it completes, then an alert with the resource details is sent to DevOps, no later than 15 minutes after detection.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-06; AC-001-06-02; BR-004; NFR-003\n- **Verification.** `residency.drift.non_eu_alerts_within_15_minutes` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCY-922826`\n\n**BE-RESIDENCY-FR-016 \u2014 Read a drift scan result**\n- Given a drift scan exists, when its result is requested by id, then its state, counts and per-resource results are returned. An unknown id is reported as not found.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-06\n- **Verification.** `residency.drift.get_returns_result_or_not_found` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCY-202775`\n\n**BE-RESIDENCY-FR-017 \u2014 A scan that cannot read the inventory never passes**\n- Given the inventory or its region metadata cannot be read, when a scan runs, then the scan ends `failed` and every gate caller treats it as blocked.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-03 (AC-001-03-03, applied to the whole inventory); US-001-05; `ASM-BE-266`\n- **Verification.** `residency.scan.unreadable_inventory_never_passes` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCY-685134`; `API-RESIDENCY-648672`; `API-RESIDENCY-922826`\n\n**BE-RESIDENCY-FR-018 \u2014 No credential or PII is stored or logged**\n- Given a backup destination or provisioning request carries a credential, when it is processed, then the credential is neither stored nor logged and only a reference is kept.\n- **Traces to.** Core section 12 (standing security constraints); NFR-018 (log safety, applied by extension)\n- **Verification.** `residency.security.no_credential_persisted_or_logged` (planned)\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCY-174124`; `API-RESIDENCY-026899`\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-RESIDENCY-174124` | POST | `/residency/backup-validations` | `residency` | 200 | 400, 422, 500 | Validate a backup destination; a non-EU destination fails the job within 30 seconds and writes no data (US-001-02, AC-001-02-02). |\n| `API-RESIDENCY-685134` | POST | `/residency/ci-gate-evaluations` | `residency` | 200 | 400, 500 | Evaluate the CI residency gate for a build; fails when any resource is outside the EU (US-001-03, BR-002). |\n| `API-RESIDENCY-913995` | GET | `/residency/compliance-report` | `residency` | 200 | 500 | Get the current compliance report of EU residency per component (US-001-04, NFR-004). |\n| `API-RESIDENCY-922826` | POST | `/residency/drift-scans` | `residency` | 201 | 500 | Run a drift scan of all resources; non-EU resources trigger an alert to DevOps within 15 minutes (US-001-06, BR-004, NFR-003). |\n| `API-RESIDENCY-202775` | GET | `/residency/drift-scans/{id}` | `residency` | 200 | 404, 500 | Get the result of a previous drift scan (US-001-06). |\n| `API-RESIDENCY-648672` | POST | `/residency/launch-gate-checks` | `residency` | 200 | 400, 500 | Run the launch gate check; refreshes the compliance report and blocks release on failure (US-001-05, BR-003, AC-001-05-02). |\n| `API-RESIDENCY-026899` | POST | `/residency/provisioning-requests` | `residency` | 201 | 400, 422, 500 | Request provisioning of a resource; refused unless the target region is an EU region (US-001-01, BR-001). |\n\n### `API-RESIDENCY-174124` \u2014 POST `/residency/backup-validations`\n\nValidate a backup destination; a non-EU destination fails the job within 30 seconds and writes no data (US-001-02, AC-001-02-02).\n\n**Request:** \n- `backup_job_id` (string, required): Identifier of the backup job being validated.\n- `destination_region` (string, required): Region of the backup destination.\n**Response:** \n- `backup_job_id` (string, required): Identifier of the validated backup job.\n- `destination_region` (string, required): Validated destination region.\n- `valid` (boolean, required): True when the destination is in an EU region.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCY-685134` \u2014 POST `/residency/ci-gate-evaluations`\n\nEvaluate the CI residency gate for a build; fails when any resource is outside the EU (US-001-03, BR-002).\n\n**Request:** \n- `build_id` (string, required): CI build identifier.\n- `resources` (array, required): Resources in the deployment, each with an identifier and region.\n**Response:** \n- `build_id` (string, required): CI build identifier evaluated.\n- `passed` (boolean, required): True when every resource is in an EU region.\n- `violations` (array, required): Resources found outside the EU; empty when passed.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCY-913995` \u2014 GET `/residency/compliance-report`\n\nGet the current compliance report of EU residency per component (US-001-04, NFR-004).\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `generated_at` (string, required): Timestamp of the last refresh.\n- `eu_verified_percent` (number, required): Share of components verified as EU-resident.\n- `components` (array, required): Each component with its region and verification status.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCY-922826` \u2014 POST `/residency/drift-scans`\n\nRun a drift scan of all resources; non-EU resources trigger an alert to DevOps within 15 minutes (US-001-06, BR-004, NFR-003).\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `scan_id` (string, required): Identifier of the scan.\n- `scanned_at` (string, required): Timestamp of the scan.\n- `all_eu` (boolean, required): True when every resource is in an EU region.\n- `non_eu_resources` (array, required): Resources found outside the EU, with details.\n- `alert_sent` (boolean, required): True when an alert was sent to the DevOps team.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCY-202775` \u2014 GET `/residency/drift-scans/{id}`\n\nGet the result of a previous drift scan (US-001-06).\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Drift scan identifier (path parameter).\n**Response:** \n- `scan_id` (string, required): Identifier of the scan.\n- `scanned_at` (string, required): Timestamp of the scan.\n- `all_eu` (boolean, required): True when every resource was in an EU region.\n- `non_eu_resources` (array, required): Resources found outside the EU.\n- `alert_sent` (boolean, required): True when an alert was sent.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCY-648672` \u2014 POST `/residency/launch-gate-checks`\n\nRun the launch gate check; refreshes the compliance report and blocks release on failure (US-001-05, BR-003, AC-001-05-02).\n\n**Request:** \n- `release_id` (string, required): Identifier of the release being gated.\n**Response:** \n- `release_id` (string, required): Release identifier checked.\n- `passed` (boolean, required): True when the release may proceed.\n- `report_generated_at` (string, required): Timestamp of the compliance report used for the check.\n- `blocking_resources` (array, required): Non-EU resources blocking release; empty when passed.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCY-026899` \u2014 POST `/residency/provisioning-requests`\n\nRequest provisioning of a resource; refused unless the target region is an EU region (US-001-01, BR-001).\n\n**Request:** \n- `resource_type` (string, required): Kind of resource to provision (database, compute, backup storage).\n- `region` (string, required): Target cloud region for the resource.\n**Response:** \n- `resource_id` (string, required): Identifier of the provisioned resource.\n- `resource_type` (string, required): Kind of resource provisioned.\n- `region` (string, required): EU region the resource was provisioned in.\n- `status` (string, required): Provisioning status.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency.invariants | status: DERIVED -->\n## Invariants\n\n- **INV-BE-251.** A `pass` result means the observed region was in `residency_approved_regions` at scan time. A NULL or unlisted region is never `pass`. (BR-001; AC-001-03-03)\n- **INV-BE-252.** A completed `drift` or `launch-gate` scan has one result row for every resource in the inventory at scan start. No resource is silently omitted. (AC-001-04-02)\n- **INV-BE-253.** A scan has verdict `pass` only if every result is `pass` and `total_count` is greater than zero. A `failed` scan has no `pass` verdict. (BR-002; BR-003; `ASM-BE-266`)\n- **INV-BE-254.** A rejected provisioning request leaves no resource created or recorded, and `resource_id` is NULL. (AC-001-01-02)\n- **INV-BE-255.** A backup validation of a non-EU destination returns failure within 30 seconds of the request and writes no backup data. (AC-001-02-02)\n- **INV-BE-256.** For a completed scan, `pass_count + fail_count + unverified_count = total_count`.\n- **INV-BE-257.** No credential, token or password is stored in any column or written to a log by this module. (Core section 12)\n- **INV-BE-258.** A scan in a terminal state (`completed`, `failed`) is never modified. A new check creates a new scan.\n- **INV-BE-259.** An alert exists only for a resource with a `fail` result in its scan, at most one per `(scan, resource)`. (AC-001-06-01; AC-001-06-02)\n- **INV-BE-260.** An `unverified` result is never counted as `pass` in any percent or verdict. (AC-001-04-02; AC-001-05-02)\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency.authorization | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Authorization\n\n**Mechanism.** How a caller authenticates (credential type, lifetime, machine identity for the pipeline and scheduler) is not decided. `backend.auth-model` is BLOCKED on `Q-BE-003`, so this module defines no mechanism (`GAP-BE-278`).\n\n**Roles.** The stories name four personas: Platform/DevOps Engineer, Release/CI Pipeline Owner, Compliance/Security Auditor and Engineering Lead. No input defines them as system roles. The matrix below is a proposal (`ASM-BE-268`). \"Machine\" means the pipeline or scheduler acting for a persona.\n\n| Operation | DevOps Engineer | CI Pipeline Owner | Auditor | Eng. Lead |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-RESIDENCY-026899` request provisioning | allow | deny | deny | deny |\n| `API-RESIDENCY-174124` validate backup destination | allow (machine) | deny | deny | deny |\n| `API-RESIDENCY-685134` evaluate CI gate | deny | allow (machine) | deny | deny |\n| `API-RESIDENCY-913995` read compliance report | allow | deny | allow | allow |\n| `API-RESIDENCY-648672` run launch gate check | deny | deny | deny | allow |\n| `API-RESIDENCY-922826` run drift scan | allow (machine) | deny | deny | deny |\n| `API-RESIDENCY-202775` read drift scan result | allow | deny | allow | allow |\n\n**Ownership predicate.** None. Residency data describes infrastructure, not user-scoped resources, so no per-record owner check exists. This is independent of `Q-BE-002`, which concerns notes.\n\nThe unauthenticated and forbidden responses depend on `Q-BE-003` and are not defined here.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## Jobs\n\n| Job | Trigger | Behaviour | Idempotency / retry |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Periodic drift scan | scheduler calling `API-RESIDENCY-922826` (AC-001-06-01 says \"periodic\") | Scans the full inventory and writes one result per resource. | Not idempotent; every call is a new scan. The period is unstated (`GAP-BE-269`). |\n| Alert dispatch sweep | on scan completion, then a sweep over `delivery_state <> 'sent'` | Sends alerts for `fail` results and sets `sent_at`. The 15-minute bound runs from `detected_at` (NFR-003; `CON-BE-256`). | Key `(scan_id, resource_id)` makes re-dispatch safe. Retry and backoff are unstated (`GAP-BE-270`). |\n| Pre-backup validation | the backup pipeline calling `API-RESIDENCY-174124` before every backup | Region check on the destination only; no data is written (`ASM-BE-270`). | Repeat calls create separate validation records; no side effect. |\n| Per-deploy CI gate | the pipeline calling `API-RESIDENCY-685134` on every deploy | Evaluates the deploy's target resources. | Each call is a new scan; the build identity is unstated (`GAP-BE-277`). |\n| Launch gate check | manual or pipeline calling `API-RESIDENCY-648672` | Runs a full scan, then computes the verdict (FR-013). | Each call is a new scan; earlier verdicts are never changed. |\n| Record retention | none | No retention period is stated for residency records. NFR-019 covers login records only (`GAP-BE-273`). | not applicable |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency.test-plan | status: DERIVED -->\n## Test plan\n\n**Unit**\n\n- Region classification: a region in the approved list gives `pass`, an unlisted region gives `fail`, a NULL region gives `fail`, and an unrecognised resource type gives `unverified` (FR-001 to FR-003, FR-008, FR-010).\n- Percent and verdict computation: all pass gives `pass`; any `fail` or `unverified` gives `fail`; zero resources gives `fail` (FR-011, FR-012; `INV-BE-253`, `INV-BE-260`).\n- Count consistency check (`INV-BE-256`).\n- Scan state guards, including rejection of a transition out of a terminal state (`INV-BE-258`).\n- Credential stripping and log redaction on destination references (FR-018; `INV-BE-257`).\n\n**Integration** (PostgreSQL 16)\n\n- The FR-001 to FR-018 scenarios end to end, with seeded fake resource inventories.\n- Backup validation timing: a non-EU destination fails within 30 seconds (FR-005; `INV-BE-255`).\n- The alert flow from a `fail` result to a `sent` alert within 15 minutes, using a fake DevOps sink (FR-015; `INV-BE-259`).\n- Unique `(scan_id, resource_id)` enforcement on alerts, and `ON DELETE RESTRICT` behaviour.\n- A report read after a launch gate check reflects that check (FR-013).\n- An unreadable inventory ends the scan `failed` and the gate blocked (FR-017).\n\n**Contract**\n\n- All seven `API-RESIDENCY-` operations in `scope:` appear in `contracts/openapi.yaml`, and no other `API-RESIDENCY-` operation does.\n- Every error response documented for these operations uses the envelope in `backend.error-model`.\n- None of these operations is classified as out of scope by the `scope-guard` checks.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency.gaps | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Gaps, contradictions and assumptions\n\nNo `Q-` is minted by this module. `ASM-BE-264` to `ASM-BE-273`, `CON-BE-255` to `CON-BE-257` and `GAP-BE-267` to `GAP-BE-279` are defined here.\n\n### Gaps\n\n| ID | Gap | Degrades | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-267` | The approved EU region list is not supplied. Only `eu-west-1` appears, and only as the email service region (NFR-017). | FR-001 to FR-008, FR-011, FR-012, `residency_approved_regions` | The list of approved region identifiers. |\n| `GAP-BE-268` | How the scanner discovers resources and reads their region is unstated. AWS services are unnamed (see `GAP-BE-012`). | `data-model`, `jobs`, FR-009, FR-014 | Platform input naming the inventory source. |\n| `GAP-BE-269` | The drift scan period is unstated (AC-001-06-01 says only \"periodic\"). | `jobs` | A schedule decision. |\n| `GAP-BE-270` | The alert channel, recipients, delivery retry and the `failed` alert handling are unstated. | State machine, FR-015, `jobs` | An alerting decision. |\n| `GAP-BE-271` | The failure signal is unstated: the response a failing gate, a refused provisioning or a backup violation gives, the residency violation code name, and how the pipeline or release process consumes the verdict. Statuses live in the operation records. | FR-002, FR-005, FR-007, FR-012 | A decision, closed together with `GAP-BE-011`. |\n| `GAP-BE-272` | It is unstated whether the provisioning operation creates the resource or only approves it for infrastructure-as-code. | FR-001, `purpose` | A decision on the provisioning operation's effect. |\n| `GAP-BE-273` | The retention and deletion of residency records are unstated. NFR-019 covers login records only. | `data-model`, `jobs` | A retention decision. |\n| `GAP-BE-274` | Behaviour is unstated when zero resources exist or no completed scan exists for the report. The fail-closed reading is `ASM-BE-266`. | FR-009, FR-011 | A decision on empty states. |\n| `GAP-BE-275` | `backend.migration-plan` has no migration for the residency tables, and `ASM-BE-011` calls the module tooling rather than request-path code. | `data-model` | A new migration entry in the project spec (`CON-BE-255`). |\n| `GAP-BE-276` | The supported backup destination forms, how the region is derived from one, and the start point of the 30-second window are unstated. The reading is `ASM-BE-270`. | FR-004, FR-005 | Backup platform input. |\n| `GAP-BE-277` | What identifies a build or deploy in a CI gate evaluation, and the format of its targets, is unstated. | `data-model` (`build_ref`), FR-006 to FR-008 | Pipeline input. |\n| `GAP-BE-278` | Caller authentication, including machine identity for the pipeline and scheduler, depends on `Q-BE-003`, and the personas are not defined as roles. | `authorization` | The `Q-BE-003` decision and a role definition. |\n| `GAP-BE-279` | No latency target is given for the report, CI gate or launch gate operations, other than the 30-second backup bound and the 15-minute alert bound. | `test-plan` | NFR input. |\n\n### Contradictions (all non-critical)\n\n| ID | Contradiction | Reading adopted | Consequence of the other side |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `CON-BE-255` | The project spec's module map and `ASM-BE-011` say `residency` is infrastructure-as-code tooling, \"not on the request path\". Seven HTTP operations are minted for it. | The operations are a control-plane surface over the tooling, served by `notes_api`. | If it is pure tooling, these operations and their tables should move out of the backend contract. |\n| `CON-BE-256` | AC-001-06-02 says \"immediately\"; NFR-003 and the operation record say \"within 15 minutes of drift detection\". | Dispatch as soon as the scan completes. 15 minutes is the upper bound. | A strict \"immediate\" would need a tighter target that no input quantifies. |\n| `CON-BE-257` | BR-003 Enforcement says \"Launch gate sign-off by Engineering Lead\" (a human act). US-001-05 and the operation say the gate is \"marked passed\" from the report. | This module computes the verdict only. Human sign-off is outside it. | If sign-off is required, a state is needed between a passing verdict and release. |\n\n### Assumptions\n\n| ID | Assumption |\n|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-264` | Tables are stored in the notes_api PostgreSQL 16 database (`platform.md`). Table names and the conventions in `data-model` (uuid ids, UTC `timestamptz`, `ON DELETE RESTRICT`) are proposals. |\n| `ASM-BE-265` | Per-resource status values are `pass`, `fail`, `unverified`. A region in the approved list is `pass`. A region not in the list, or missing or unreadable (AC-001-03-03), is `fail`. A resource type the scanner does not recognise is `unverified` (AC-001-04-02). `unverified` counts as not EU-verified and stays in the denominator. |\n| `ASM-BE-266` | Percent compliant is `pass_count / total_count` over all resources. Zero resources, an unreadable inventory or a `failed` scan never passes any gate (fail-closed). |\n| `ASM-BE-267` | The residency violation error uses the code `RESIDENCY_VIOLATION`, following the proposed envelope of `ASM-BE-007`. |\n| `ASM-BE-268` | The persona-to-operation matrix in `authorization` is a proposal. The pipeline and scheduler call as machine identities of the matching persona. |\n| `ASM-BE-269` | One alert is raised per `(scan, resource)` with a `fail` result. It is dispatched at scan completion, and the 15 minutes run from `detected_at`. |\n| `ASM-BE-270` | Backup validation is a region check on the destination, done before any write. The 30 seconds are measured from receipt of the request to the failure response. |\n| `ASM-BE-271` | A resource is identified by the provider's `external_ref`. A CI-gate target not yet in the inventory is inserted with the region as supplied. |\n| `ASM-BE-272` | Scan and alert state changes are made only by this module. An illegal transition is rejected internally and logged, with no API status. |\n| `ASM-BE-273` | CI-gate evaluations cover a deploy's targets only. The compliance report reads the latest completed `launch-gate` or `drift` scan, never a `ci-gate` scan. Reading the report never starts a scan. Every gate check creates a new scan, and prior verdicts are never changed. |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-001-01", "US-001-02", "US-001-03", "US-001-04", "US-001-05", "US-001-06"]}, {"id": "backend/contracts/openapi.yaml", "type": "openapi", "path": "backend/contracts/openapi.yaml", "content": "openapi: 3.1.0\ninfo:\n  title: Backend API\n  version: 1.0.0\n  description: Operations the backend module specifications define, rendered from\n    their structured operation records. None was inferred.\npaths:\n  /auth/request-code:\n    post:\n      operationId: API-AUTH-015365\n      summary: Request a 6-digit one-time login code to be emailed to the given address.\n        Limited to 3 requests within 10 minutes; allowed while verification is locked\n        (BR-032).\n      description: Defined by module `auth` in `modules/auth-SPEC.md`.\n      requestBody:\n        required: true\n        content:\n          application/json:\n            schema:\n              type: object\n              additionalProperties: false\n              required:\n              - email\n              properties:\n                email:\n                  description: Email address to send the one-time login code to (BR-020,\n                    ASM-BE-001; trimmed and compared case-insensitively).\n                  type: string\n      responses:\n        '202':\n          description: Request a 6-digit one-time login code to be emailed to the\n            given address. Limited to 3 requests within 10 minutes; allowed while\n            verification is locked (BR-032).\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                type: object\n                additionalProperties: false\n                required:\n                - message\n                properties:\n                  message:\n                    description: Confirmation that a code has been sent, without revealing\n                      the code or an unmasked email address (NFR-018).\n                    type: string\n                  expires_in_seconds:\n                    description: Seconds until the issued code expires; 10 minutes\n                      per BR-020.\n                    type: integer\n        '400':\n          description: See `modules/auth-SPEC.md`, API-AUTH-015365.\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                $ref: '#/components/schemas/AuthError'\n        '429':\n          description: See `modules/auth-SPEC.md`, API-AUTH-015365.\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                $ref: '#/components/schemas/AuthError'\n        '500':\n          description: See `modules/auth-SPEC.md`, API-AUTH-015365.\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                $ref: '#/components/schemas/AuthError'\n      x-devagent-source:\n        module: auth\n        document: modules/auth-SPEC.md\n        id: API-AUTH-015365\n  /auth/verify-code:\n    post:\n      operationId: API-AUTH-592570\n      summary: 'Verify a one-time login code. Codes are single use, expire after 10\n        minutes, and 5 wrong codes lock verification for 15 minutes (BR-033). Refusal\n        precedence: lock, used, expired, wrong (ASM-BE-003).'\n      description: Defined by module `auth` in `modules/auth-SPEC.md`.\n      requestBody:\n        required: true\n        content:\n          application/json:\n            schema:\n              type: object\n              additionalProperties: false\n              required:\n              - email\n              - code\n              properties:\n                email:\n                  description: Email address the code was issued to (ASM-BE-001).\n                  type: string\n                code:\n                  description: The 6-digit numeric one-time code (BR-029).\n                  type: string\n      responses:\n        '200':\n          description: 'Verify a one-time login code. Codes are single use, expire\n            after 10 minutes, and 5 wrong codes lock verification for 15 minutes (BR-033).\n            Refusal precedence: lock, used, expired, wrong (ASM-BE-003).'\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                type: object\n                additionalProperties: false\n                required:\n                - verified\n                properties:\n                  verified:\n                    description: True when the code was accepted. the service assigns a unique ID,\n        timestamps and pinned=false by default (US-002-01, US-006-02; BR-005, BR-018).\n      description: Defined by module `notes` in `modules/notes-SPEC.md`.\n      requestBody:\n        required: true\n        content:\n          application/json:\n            schema:\n              type: object\n              additionalProperties: false\n              required:\n              - title\n              - body\n              properties:\n                title:\n                  description: Note title; required, missing or empty returns 400\n                    (BR-005).\n                  type: string\n                body:\n                  description: Note body; required, missing or empty returns 400 (BR-005).\n                  type: string\n                pinned:\n                  description: Initial pinned flag; defaults to false when omitted\n                    (US-002-01, BR-018).\n                  type: boolean\n      responses:\n        '201':\n          description: Create a note with a title and body; the service assigns a\n            unique ID, timestamps and pinned=false by default (US-002-01, US-006-02;\n            BR-005, BR-018).\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                type: object\n                additionalProperties: false\n                required:\n                - id\n                - title\n                - body\n                - pinned\n                - status\n                - tags\n                - created_at\n                - updated_at\n                properties:\n                  id:\n                    description: Unique note ID assigned by the service (US-002-01).\n                    type: string\n                  title:\n                    description: Note title.\n                    type: string\n                  body:\n                    description: Note body.\n                    type: string\n                  pinned:\n                    description: Pinned flag; false by default.\n                    type: boolean\n                  status:\n                    description: Active or archived status of the note (US-002-05,\n                      US-002-06); a new note is active.\n                    type: string\n                  tags:\n                    description: Tags carried by the note; empty on creation.\n                    type: array\n                  created_at:\n                    description: Creation timestamp (US-002-01).\n                    type: string\n                  updated_at:\n                    description: Last-update timestamp (US-002-01).\n                    type: string\n        '400':\n          description: See `modules/notes-SPEC.md`, API-NOTES-232390.\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                $ref: '#/components/schemas/NotesError'\n        '415':\n          description: See `modules/notes-SPEC.md`, API-NOTES-232390.\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                $ref: '#/components/schemas/NotesError'\n      x-devagent-source:\n        module: notes\n        document: modules/notes-SPEC.md\n        id: API-NOTES-232390\n  /notes/search:\n    get:\n      operationId: API-SEARCH-580505\n      summary: Search notes by keyword, by tag, or by both combined. Results are paginated,\n        exclude archived notes unless include_archived is set, and list pinned notes\n        first. Returns 503 when the search index is degraded. At least one of q or\n        tags is required (ASM-BE-009, CON-BE-003).\n      description: Defined by module `search` in `modules/search-SPEC.md`.\n      parameters:\n      - name: q\n        in: query\n        required: false\n        description: Keyword matched against note title and body. At least one of\n          q or tags must be supplied. A present but empty q returns 400 (US-003-01).\n        schema:\n          type: string\n      - name: tags\n        in: query\n        required: false\n        description: One or more tag names to filter by. When combined with q, both\n          filters apply (US-003-02, US-003-04). Tag rules are open (GAP-BE-010).\n        schema:\n          type: array\n      - name: include_archived\n        in: query\n        required: false\n        description: When true, archived notes are included. The default is false,\n          so archived notes are excluded (BR-009). A non-boolean value returns 400.\n        schema:\n          type: boolean\n      - name: page\n        in: query\n        required: false\n        description: 1-based page number. An invalid or non-numeric value returns\n          400 (US-003-05).\n        schema:\n          type: integer\n      - name: page_size\n        in: query\n        required: false\n        description: Results per page, maximum 100 (NFR-007). The default of 20 is\n          an assumption (ASM-BE-009). A value over the maximum or a non-numeric value\n          returns 400.\n        schema:\n          type: integer\n      responses:\n        '200':\n          description: Search notes by keyword, by tag, or by both combined. Results\n            are paginated, exclude archived notes unless include_archived is set,\n            and list pinned notes first. Returns 503 when the search index is degraded.\n            At least one of q or tags is required (ASM-BE-009, CON-BE-003).\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                type: object\n                additionalProperties: false\n                required:\n                - items\n                - page\n                - page_size\n                - total\n                properties:\n                  items:\n                    description: Matching notes for the requested page, with pinned\n                      notes before unpinned ones (BR-010). Each item shows the note's\n                      id, title, body, pinned flag, status (active or archived), tags\n                      and timestamps.\n                    type: array\n                  page:\n                    description: The page number returned.\n                    type: integer\n                  page_size:\n                    description: The page size applied.\n                    type: integer\n                  total:\n                    description: Total number of notes matching the filters across\n                      all pages.\n                    type: integer\n        '400':\n          description: See `modules/search-SPEC.md`, API-SEARCH-580505.\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                $ref: '#/components/schemas/SearchError'\n        '503':\n          description: See `modules/search-SPEC.md`, API-SEARCH-580505.\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                $ref: '#/components/schemas/SearchError'\n        '500':\n          description: See `modules/search-SPEC.md`, API-SEARCH-580505.\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                $ref: '#/components/schemas/SearchError'\n      x-devagent-source:\n        module: search\n        document: modules/search-SPEC.md\n        id: API-SEARCH-580505\n  /notes/{id}:\n    get:\n      operationId: API-NOTES-045877\n      summary: Get a single note by ID, including its pinned flag, status and tags\n        (US-002-06, US-006-02, US-006-05).\n      description: Defined by module `notes` in `modules/notes-SPEC.md`.\n      parameters:\n      - Kind | What is needed | Why | Blocking | Waiting sections |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `HANDOFF-NOTESAPI-067561` | `business_rule` | Provide a clean statement of BR-030: who may request, verify or receive a login code (any email address, or only a known set). | Without it the auth actors and access rules cannot be written (Q-BE-001). | yes | `backend.auth-model`, `backend.api-surface` |\n| `HANDOFF-NOTESAPI-217437` | `business_rule` | Decide whether notes are owned per user or shared in the team workspace, and give the access rule for each note operation (BR-031). | It sets the access predicate on every note read and write and decides whether ownership columns exist (Q-BE-002). | yes | `backend.auth-model`, `backend.domain-model`, `backend.migration-plan` |\n| `HANDOFF-NOTESAPI-632796` | `business_rule` | Decide what a successful code verification issues (credential type, lifetime, transport, refresh, logout) and whether note and search endpoints require it. | The verify response and 401/403 behaviour on all other endpoints depend on it (Q-BE-003). | yes | `backend.auth-model`, `backend.api-surface`, `backend.error-model` |\n| `HANDOFF-NOTESAPI-413008` | `story` | Provide a story or decision for operations that list active notes (pinned first) and archived-only notes. | Search requires q or a tag, so US-006-01 and the archived view have no backend operation (GAP-BE-005). | no | `backend.api-surface`, `backend.coverage` |\n| `HANDOFF-NOTESAPI-998344` | `story` | Provide a story or decision for an operation listing distinct tags, together with tag rules (case, charset, length, maximum per note). | The tag filter and tags list have no data source and tag validation is open (GAP-BE-006, GAP-BE-010). | no | `backend.api-surface`, `backend.coverage` |\n| `HANDOFF-NOTESAPI-352541` | `business_rule` | Decide remaining lock time on 423, Retry-After values, counter reset rule, whether verify is rate-limited, and the final error envelope and code names. | Lockout and refusal responses cannot be fully specified (GAP-BE-008, GAP-BE-011). | no | `backend.error-model`, `backend.api-surface` |\n\n## Needs answered here\n\n| Need | From lane | Asked | Answered in | How |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `HANDOFF-WEBAPP-452996` | `web_app` | Specify the remaining lock time, the re-enable rule, precedence among simultaneous verify refusals, whether a new code can be requested while locked, and the final error-code registry. | `backend.error-model` | Proposes the error-code registry and verify-refusal precedence (lock, used, expired, wrong), the 423 lock status, and that a code may be requested during a lock; remaining lock time and re-enable detail stay open as GAP-BE-008 and GAP-BE-011. |\n\n## Needs this lane cannot answer\n\n| Need | From lane | Asked | Why not |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `HANDOFF-WEBAPP-500592` | `web_app` | Decide and define the session model: what a successful code verification issues (credential type, lifetime, transport), refresh and logout operations, and whether notes and search endpoints require a session (Q-BE-003). | The session model is Q-BE-003, a blocking human decision. No input decides the credential, lifetime, refresh, logout or whether notes and search require a session. |\n| `HANDOFF-WEBAPP-732181` | `web_app` | Add operations to list active notes and to list archived-only notes, since search requires q or a tag and include_archived mixes both states. | No story or decision defines list operations for active or archived-only notes (GAP-BE-005). Adding them would be inventing scope that needs a human decision. |\n| `HANDOFF-WEBAPP-263975` | `web_app` | Add an operation that lists distinct tags, usable for the tag filter options and the tags list screen. | No story or decision defines a distinct-tags operation, and tag rules are open (GAP-BE-006, GAP-BE-010). It needs a human decision. |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.handoffs -->\n"}], "notices": []}
